# PLAN — Executing the Cancer-Dependency RSI Research Loop (v2)

**Specification:** `cancer_rsi_handoff.txt` (28 Oct 2026) · **Locked goal:** `GOAL.md` · **Project root:** `cancer-dependency-rsi/`
**Host (verified 2026-10-28):** Apple M5 Max, 18 cores, 128 GB RAM, 532 GiB free, macOS 26.6 arm64; Python 3.14.8.
**Model access:** Muse `gpt-6.1-sol` (config default `high`); host agent for lead work. All scientific fitting, simulation, evaluation, and data processing run **locally on this laptop**; hosted frontier-model reasoning is used only through the already-authorized host/codex interface (spec §2).
**Review state:** v1 scored 53/100 (sol-high, 35 issues, all verified+fixed — `reviews/review_r2_verdict.md`, `reviews/review_r2_dispositions.md`). This v2 is the corrected execution contract.

Sequencing invariant: **no outcome-bearing import before provisional partitions + quarantine; no adaptive search before identifiability (G1); no policy optimization before the frozen evaluator (G2/Lock 1); no final outcome touched before Lock 2.** Failing a gate narrows the question or ends honestly — never lowers the standard.

## 0. Operating rules (bind every phase)

### 0.1 Model routing & the frozen generator protocol (user-mandated)
| Role | Model | Effort | Pattern |
|---|---|---|---|
| Adversarial review of **everything** | `gpt-6.1-sol` | `high` | `codex exec --skip-git-repo-check -m gpt-6.1-sol -c model_reasoning_effort='"high"' -s read-only` |
| Generation (candidates, challengers, summaries, extraction, unstick) | `gpt-6.1-sol` | `xhigh` | same, `xhigh`, sandbox per task |
| Lead investigator / statistics / implementation | Host agent | — | Local; all computation local |

- **Review protocol:** severity-tagged issues (low/medium/high/critical) with file:line evidence + one 0–100 score → lead verifies each (accept/reject with reason) → fix all verified → re-review until **≥ 90 and zero verified high/critical**. Transcripts + dispositions in `reviews/` + review ledger.
- **Generator-protocol freeze (isolates policy improvement):** for any policy comparison, the *entire* generation protocol is frozen and versioned — proposer prompt, direction-summary prompt, catalog-extraction prompt+schema, candidate ranking inputs, repair/unstick allowances, model IDs + efforts. Fixed arms run the **same protocol on their own admissible histories**. Any discretionary human/agent intervention outside the frozen protocol is logged as a **protocol change** (starts a new comparison epoch) or the affected experiments are **excluded** from the comparison. Only the executable **policy module** differs between arms.
- **Context/retrieval contract:** the proposer receives (a) an **exhaustive history index** (every attempt: ID, direction, origin, parents, outcome class, quality, cost — no omission), (b) the episode-admissible catalog/frontier view (§0.4.2), (c) the current direction summary, (d) retrieved full records for index entries it cites. Retrieval is deterministic (cited IDs only, logged); context-limit handling = index always complete, full records by citation, truncation never silent (overflow ⇒ generation splits by direction). This is the single definition of "full tried history" used in GOAL.md.
- Specialist roles stay distinct (novelty, provenance, modeling, evaluator, adversarial review). The evaluator gets a narrower interface than the proposer — a logged protocol, **not** a cryptographic boundary, never described as one.
- Every model call ledgered: model, effort, exact prompt (retained, §0.3), tokens when exposed, wall/CPU, peak RSS, cost or `unknown`. Additional metered API cap = **$0** unless user-configured; unmeasurable material cost ⇒ C9 all-in savings uncertifiable (Phase 5).

### 0.2 Resource envelope & worst-case reservations (`config/resources.json`, enforced in code)
- No GPU; ≤ 2 CPU-heavy jobs; research RSS ≤ min(8 GiB, 60% RAM) = **8 GiB**; exploratory timeout **600 s** (longer only via recorded campaign allocation); downloads ≤ **20 GiB**; pilot = 24 nodes + 3 challengers + incumbent, ≤ 8 h; **campaign allocations** (total wall time, node count, model-call count per phase) fixed at Lock 1 within these ceilings. Ceilings never auto-increase.
- **Reservation rules (spec §4, acceptance-tested):** before any dispatch, reserve the **conservative billable maximum** (not an estimate); concurrent reservations and **retry reservations count** against the cap; the reservation record is **persisted+fsynced before dispatch**; interrupted ⇒ stays `reserved` until reconciled against returned usage; **never dispatch to a provider whose billable maximum cannot be bounded** by config; completed calls never repeated on resume. Tests must cover concurrent requests, retries, and interrupted reservations. (Local compute jobs reserve wall/RSS slots the same way with $0 cost.)

### 0.3 Persistence, provenance retention & honesty primitives
- Append-only JSONL ledgers (`experiments`, `resources`, `reviews`, `claims`); node states `proposed → dispatched → completed|failed → committed`; checkpoint after every completed experiment.
- **Retained provenance (hashes are indexes, not substitutes):** a content-addressed artifact store (`artifacts/sha256/…`) retains, immutably, for every node: artifact bytes; **exact rendered prompt**; **canonical generator-input manifest** (every input artifact ID+hash, incl. parent artifacts, catalog/summary/frontier snapshot IDs, budget state, rejection history shown); **action/observation event sequence** (policy inputs/outputs in order); selecting **policy ID+version+state**; generator model+effort+seed; data/split/evaluator hashes **and versions**; execution-environment manifest (env lock hash, host fingerprint); result, validity diagnostics, failures, measured costs.
- `STATUS.md` + `status.json`: phase, incumbent, spend, three separate status fields (`scientific_discovery`, `prediction_improvement`, `policy_improvement`), unmet C1–C13 gates, next action, exact resume command. "Running" claimed only with live PID/job + heartbeat.
- Terminal states only from GOAL. Failed final test stays failed; reuse of its outcomes = exploratory.

### 0.4 Trajectory system (Dream-RSI adaptation — departures disclosed)

Adapted from Dream-RSI (arXiv:2609.14858; `google-research/dream-rsi`): discovery history as replay simulator; offline policy improvement ("dreaming") then online redeployment; fixed generator+evaluator+task, only policy changes. Spec constraints override the paper where they conflict; then the method is labeled **Dream-RSI-inspired adaptation**.

**0.4.1 Units & DAG schema.** *Node/attempt* = one evaluated artifact. Every node has an **ordered parent array** (possibly empty, one, or many), forming an **acyclic** lineage DAG validated at insert. Each parent edge records the parent's immutable contribution (which components carried over). Nodes carry `study_id`, `evaluator_version`, `world_id` (the round/tree that produced them; recombination children belong to their birth world and reference parents across worlds by ID). **Replay eligibility:** a node is replayable in an episode only if (a) every information-bearing parent is either revealed earlier in the episode or in the initially-available set, (b) study/evaluator versions match the episode, and (c) its canonical input manifest matches the episode state (§0.4.6). **Ancestry cost** = cost of the union of required ancestor nodes, charged **once** per episode (Phase 5 allocation). *Branch/trajectory* = the primary-parent (first-parent) chain view of the DAG, used for best-so-far curves; *discovery tree* = one round's forest under one policy on one panel, frozen+hashed into the **trace pool** as a replay world (pool only grows). Tree shape `BRANCHES × (REFINE+1)` scaled to budget; exact values frozen at Lock 1. *Direction* = coherent research line over many nodes.

*Identities (dedup done right):* `attempt_id` (each execution) · `context_hash` (generator-input manifest hash) · `config_id` (hash of the artifact's scientific configuration) · `evaluation_id` (config × data × split × evaluator version). Re-running an existing `evaluation_id` is a **replication** (allowed only as a prespecified replication/repair with reason, counted separately); same context but new config = legitimate new attempt; same config new context = duplicate configuration (rejected unless authorized replication). Trial accounting uses these levels (§0.5.1.8).

**0.4.2 Direction catalog with episode views.** `directions/catalog.json` + immutable snapshots (`history/iterNNN.json`). Status is **four independent fields**, each with deterministic predicates in a **total transition table frozen at Lock 1** (every state combination + missing-evidence + precedence + recovery transitions defined; each transition cites evidence node IDs): `depth` ∈ {unexplored, underexplored, explored, well_explored} · `promise` ∈ {unknown, unpromising, promising} · `fragility` ∈ {unknown, robust, fragile} · `recoverability` ∈ {n/a, recoverable, dead}. Derived labels (e.g. "saturated" = depth well_explored + marginal-gain < ε over last k attempts) are **resource-allocation judgments, never scientific exhaustion verdicts**. Catalog also stores: evidence attempt IDs, best/median quality, quality-vs-cost slope, failure taxonomy, `avoid_repeating`, bottlenecks. Extraction (sol-xhigh, schema-validated) proposes classifications; **deterministic predicates verify them** against recorded outcomes — mismatches rejected+ledgered.
**Episode views (no future leakage):** anything fed to a policy/generator during replay — catalog snapshot, frontier, summaries, priorities, legal actions — is **recomputed from admissible history only**: complete earlier worlds + the revealed prefix of the current world. Global/current catalog objects never enter replay. Acceptance test: mutating any unrevealed outcome must not change any episode-view artifact or policy decision (hidden-outcome invariance test).

**0.4.3 Trajectory scoring (formal, deterministic, no LLM).** Locked at Lock 1 with formulas; defaults: per-node **valid quality** `q ∈ [0,1]` = locked monotone transform of the primary scientific endpoint (higher = better; sign/direction fixed in contract; invalid ⇒ no q, counted invalid, never 0-as-quality). Per trajectory with cumulative measured cost `c`: best-valid-so-far `B(c) = max q over valid nodes with cumulative cost ≤ c` (0 if none); **cost-to-threshold** `C(τ) = min c with B(c) ≥ τ`, else **censored at budget** (never an invented number); **normalized area-over-curve** `AoC = (1/B_ep) ∫₀^{B_ep} B(c) dc` over the episode budget `B_ep`; validity rate; **robustness** `R` = locked sensitivity statistic (e.g. min quality retained across prespecified sensitivity replicates, formula at Lock 1). **Pareto frontier** computed **within a comparable domain** (same task panel + evaluator version + endpoint) over (final B, total cost, R). Precedence: frontier membership = evidence quality; saturation/pruning = allocation status — a nondominated-but-saturated trajectory stays frontier-listed but allocation-paused; neither label overwrites the other.

**0.4.4 Origins & generation.** Sol-xhigh proposes candidates under the frozen protocol (§0.1); each declares exactly one node-operation/origin:
- `new` (0 parents; opens unexplored direction) · `refinement` (exactly 1 parent; mutate/extend a frontier or winner artifact) · `underexplored_extension` (1+ parents in a thin-evidence direction) · `combination` (≥2 parents; each contribution stated; legality of each element checked; revalidated from scratch; no score inheritance) · `repair` (≥1 parent + cited failure node ID + named fix).
Runner rejects (ledgered, fed back next round): outside locked spaces; duplicate `config_id` without replication authorization; nonexistent parents/evidence; `avoid_repeating` repeats without `repair` justification; cyclic or version-incompatible lineage.

**0.4.5 Selection = executable policy over prefix-recomputed state.** Proposal (sol-xhigh: what *could* run + priorities as inputs) ≠ selection (versioned `policy(episode_view, legal_actions, remaining_budget) → action`). **Legal actions are recomputed at each step from the revealed prefix** using frozen transition/pruning rules — historical legal-action sequences are audit records, never counterfactual state for challengers. Keep/prune: frontier trajectories refinable; allocation-paused (saturated) directions leave the legal set until a frozen reactivation rule fires; `fragile` trajectories refinable only via prespecified robustness diagnostics. All recomputation is deterministic from the episode view (replayable bit-for-bit given the same revealed outcomes).

**0.4.6 Dreaming: replay objective, ρ, exact context, common support.**
- **ρ is a budget-control parameter** (as in the paper): replay evaluates each policy at operating budgets `ρ·B_ep` for ρ ∈ {0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1}. **Replay objective (complete formula frozen at Lock 1; default):** per episode e and ρ, `J_e(π,ρ) = B_e(ρ·B_ep)` (best valid quality attained within that budget; invalid nodes contribute cost, no quality; zero-attainment ⇒ 0). Policy score `J(π) = Σ_ρ w_ρ · mean_e J_e(π,ρ)` with locked weights `w_ρ` (default uniform), ties broken by lower mean cost-to-τ then incumbent preference. Costs are measured ledger costs (§0.4.1 ancestry rule); endpoints' signs/scales per contract.
- **Exact context (operational):** refinement generation is **parent-local by construction** — the generator's inputs are exactly its canonical manifest (parent artifact(s) + frozen instruction + episode-view snapshot ID + budget token). A recorded child replays **iff** the challenger's episode state reproduces that manifest hash exactly; **any differing input ⇒ `unsupported`**, never approximated.
- **Common support (frozen at Lock 1):** comparison set = frozen list of episodes (world × panel × seed); **coverage** = supported decisions / all decision points; **eligibility threshold** (default: ≥ 80% supported decisions per policy and ≥ 8 complete episodes per policy, else replay is *inadmissible as a screen* for that comparison → fresh rollouts decide); an unsupported action **terminates the episode as `unsupported`** — its quality is excluded from means, its cost counts in coverage reporting, and it can **never** count as a cheap successful stop. Policies compared **paired on identical episode sets**; aggregation weights locked; support coverage always reported beside scores.
- Both arms share the same first round **E0** (starting policy; copied read-only; its production cost charged per Phase 5 allocation — copying is not free).

**0.4.7 Audit views.** `status --trajectories`: lineage DAG (all parents), direction board (4-field statuses), frontier data, generation-over-generation table, allocation-paused list + reasons, coverage reports. Derived deterministically from ledgers; ledger wins on disagreement (= view bug).

### 0.5 Generation protocol (adapted from the business LOOP, hardened)

Modeled on `~/bufferright/business/data/research/paths/LOOP/` (48 generations; "Sol xhigh proposes, Sol high reviews"; score trails 54→76→92). Translation: sleeve pool → analysis-family pool; blend → `combination`; PREREG hash → SPEC hash; forward ledger → frozen predictions; anchor → evaluator-compatibility suite (below). **Two separate lifecycles** (never mixed): **(S) scientific-artifact generations** and **(P) policy generations**, each with own SPEC, screen, battery, denominators, trial counters.

**0.5.1 Anatomy (`generations/{s,p}genNNN/`, never reused/overwritten).**
1. **SPEC.md + SHA-256 locked in ledger before any run:** discriminating question, candidate families, screen definition (incl. whether it is a *necessary-condition* screen — §0.5.2), battery definition, pass bar, stop rules, reserved battery budget. Amendments = new versioned files; locked-science amendments follow new-study-ID rule.
2. **Enumerate** from the pool under frozen protocol; origins+parents per §0.4.4; legality validated.
3. **Screen (ranking only, labeled approximate).** Screen-positives are never claims.
4. **Recombine/refine winners** (`combination`/`refinement`); re-enter at screen; no score inheritance.
5. **Battery (decisive):** (S) full frozen evaluator + validity + matched controls + ablations on fresh development rollouts; (P) Phase 4/5 procedure (replay screen → fresh paired rollouts incl. incumbent). Battery budget is **reserved before screening**; if ceilings intervene, unfinished batteries are `PENDING`, never silently dropped.
6. **Verdicts are scoped to what was tested:** `ADVANCES` · `FAILS-BATTERY (tested finalists: n/N, with numbers)` · `FAMILY-CLOSED` (allowed **only** with a proven necessary-condition screen or exhaustive battery — §0.5.2; else untested members remain explicitly open) · `INVALID` (defective record preserved, corrected in a new version) · `BLOCKED-DATA` (no proxy substitution without claim downgrade) · `PENDING`.
7. **Sol-high review → verify → fix → re-review to ≥ 90**, per-issue dispositions (`CONFIRMED+FIXED` / `ACCEPTED with claim downgrade` / `REJECTED with evidence`).
8. **`GENERATIONS.md` entry:** numbers for every finalist incl. failures, lessons, next mandate, stop-rule checks (leakage ⇒ dependent lines cancelled), and **multi-counter trial accounting** (cumulative, separate: unique configs, attempts, replications, hypothesis inspections, policy candidates, replay episodes, batteries — scientific vs engineering adaptation never merged into one total; no count substitutes for confirmatory procedure).

**0.5.2 Screening honesty.** A screen justifies exhaustive rejection **only** if passing it is a mathematically necessary condition for passing the battery (proof stated in SPEC, e.g. battery metric is a monotone refinement bounded by the screen metric). Otherwise verdicts read "no *tested finalist* passes" and screen-negative members stay listed as untested. Cheapest-falsification probes run as one fixed SPEC with no variants; failure closes only the tested specification, with family-level closure requiring the necessary-condition argument.

**0.5.3 Evaluator versioning (anchor is not certification).** Scores from different evaluator versions are **never pooled** in one comparison/replay pool. The frozen baseline "anchor" reproduction is a smoke check only. Any evaluator change requires: compatibility suite (rankings + validity decisions on a frozen probe set), explicit invalidation list for affected results, and — if substantive — the new-study/new-evidence procedure. Versions preserved side-by-side.

**0.5.4 Matched controls & post-hoc rule.** A new ingredient is credited only vs a same-construction control lacking it; unmatched construction changes are disclosed as non-decomposable. Post-hoc discoveries (e.g. control beats hypothesis) are flagged post-hoc, may be frozen for forward judging, never promotable on consumed data.

**0.5.5 Generation boundary (end-to-end).** Deterministic scores/frontier/episode views → sol-xhigh direction summary + ranked proposal (keep / recombine-with-rationale / repair / open / pause, all ID-cited) → policy code executes selection under budget → runs → extraction + predicate verification → sol-high ≥ 90 → ledger + trace-pool append.

### 0.6 Autonomy, unstick protocol, campaign limits & terminal determination
- **Default = continue autonomously** through failures/negative results, generation after generation, **within campaign allocations** (Lock 1: per-phase + total wall/nodes/model-calls, inside §0.2 ceilings). Legitimate stops: hard authorization gate (credential/spending/required file), allocation/ceiling exhaustion, explicit user stop, no-progress determination (below), terminal determination.
- **Unstick (sol-xhigh):** on stall (no obvious legal action, unexpected gate failure, all directions paused/dead/blocked, contradictory results, same failure twice) invoke sol-xhigh with the state packet (STATUS, episode views, frontier, last 3 generation entries, blocker + attempts) for ranked unblocks + cheapest discriminating experiment + draft next SPEC. Verify vs locked contract (sol cannot authorize contract/ceiling/final-access/standard changes); ledger consultation+disposition; **unstick calls are charged costs** (Phase 5). Max 3 consultation rounds per blocker, then smallest legal evidence-gathering action. Under a frozen comparison, unstick input is restricted to that arm's admissible history (§0.1 freeze).
- **No-progress / negative-EV rule (prevents indefinite retries, spec §14):** if K consecutive generations (K at Lock 1, default 3) produce no frontier movement, no new identifiable question, and no gate progress, sol-xhigh + lead jointly assess; concurrence ⇒ admissible **non-complete terminal determination** (`INCONCLUSIVE`/`BLOCKED` as applicable) with unmet gates + smallest next step. Global retry limits per failure mode also frozen at Lock 1.
- **Terminal determination (sol-xhigh judges, evidence rules):** `COMPLETE_*` requires sol-xhigh's written gate-by-gate (C1–C13) certification over the full evidence package, score ≥ 90, zero unresolved high/critical — **in addition to** the evidence, never instead of it; it cannot waive gates. A **pre-reserved terminal-review budget slice** (Lock 1) guarantees the call is affordable; if it still cannot run, the executor may mechanically checkpoint and report `BLOCKED`/`BUDGET_EXHAUSTED` honestly — but **positive completion always requires the sol-xhigh determination**. Non-complete determinations list exact unmet gates; consumed Layer-B/C evidence stays consumed (Phases 5–6).

Create `cancer-dependency-rsi/` (README, GOAL copy+hash, PROTOCOL stub, STATUS+status.json, `config/{resources,host}.json`, `src/`, `data/`, `ledger/`, `reviews/`, `artifacts/`, `generations/`, `directions/`, `trace_pool/`, `tests/`, `manuscript/`); project `.venv` via uv + lockfile; implement first: budget wrapper (§0.2 tests incl. concurrency/retries/interruption), append-only ledgers, artifact store, hashing, CLI skeleton (`bootstrap, audit-data, reproduce-baselines, run-pilot, run-cycle, evaluate-policy, lock-science, evaluate-external, reproduce, status, resume` — gate-locked commands fail loudly naming the gate). Record host fingerprint without secrets. **G0:** status/resume work; dummy over-budget job demonstrably killed (timeout+RSS); tests green; sol-high ≥ 90. *(Status 2026-10-28: scaffold + ledger/budget + 3 tests done; CLI + remaining tests pending.)*

## Phase 1 — Quarantine-first verification & identifiability audit (Gate G1 → C1–C3)
**1a. Metadata-only first.** Before importing any outcome-bearing file: create **provisional A/B/C partitions from metadata alone** (model/donor/lineage lists, no outcome values), write the **quarantine + exposure log** (every outcome-file access records who/what/when/which partition). Permitted pre-Lock-1 outcome contact: **Layer-A (development) sources only** (incl. the Broad minipool + genome-wide development data) and *published* QC summaries for B/C candidates. Sanger outcome matrices, once imported, sit quarantined as Layer-C candidates: schema/shape/control-gene inventory checks allowed via logged scripts; **no outcome-value inspection, ranking, or modeling** until Lock 2. Any accidental exposure is logged and downgrades affected claims.
**1b. Read primary sources in full** (S1 PMC13581591, S2 PMC13581617, S3 PMC13664187 + Zenodo 22472622, S5 arXiv:2609.14858 + repo): exact designs, selection rules, score definitions/directions, QC, published findings (= controls). *(Done at full-text level 2026-10-28; supplements/code deep-read continues.)*
**1c. Routes via official manifests only** — source URL, release/commit, date, license, bytes, SHA-256 per file into `data/source_manifest*.json`. Never invent URLs; never bypass challenges (DepMap portal verification page ⇒ use study-specific figshare releases, preferred anyway). **Leakage rule (spec §5, corrected):** if any correction/normalization/**target or model selection** used external outcomes, that evidence requires **development-only reconstruction or genuinely independent validation — disclosure never repairs it**; otherwise the independence gate stays **unmet**. Only **covariate-only** joint preprocessing may proceed as disclosed transductive preprocessing with limited claims. Audit Chronos pre-training + 26Q3 integration under this rule.
**1d. Provenance + crosswalk + counts:** model/donor/lineage/origin/lab/library/medium/format/replicate/release/outcome; Broad↔HCMI↔Sanger↔author IDs; uncertain donor = `uncertain`; count **independent models/donors** (genes/guides/wells/cultures ≠ patients); attrition registry. Verify seeds at row level. *(Minipool verified: 7 models, 18 screens, 5 medium + 7 format matched contrasts, 250 selected genes, 0 missing; ACH-003052 lineage gap + HCM-SANG↔ACH crosswalk open.)*
**1e. Assay audit (Layer-A + published summaries):** Chronos vs Sanger LFC/BAGEL — direction, range, controls, missingness, QC per source; absolute values never interchanged/subtracted. Confirm required pairings exist before modeling; check lab↔biology confounding.
**1f. Novelty audit v1:** dated search (queries/exclusions preserved) + claim ledger (nearest prior, established part, precise residual uncertainty, evidence needed, consequences). Rule out generic 2D→organoid transfer (S3) and published format/medium class findings (S1) as novelty.
**Gate G1:** named falsifiable question in a named subset + counted independent units + documented paired contrasts (or documented narrower question; broader component recorded unmet). No adaptive search before G1. Sol-high ≥ 90 on provenance + novelty package.

## Phase 2 — Vertical slice, baselines, Lock 1 (Gate G2 → C4–C5)
1. **Estimators (Layer-A only):** paired within-model contrasts in original units — both conditions, **all individual paired changes**, uncertainty, heterogeneity. **Joint uncertainty is mandatory:** model-level (cluster) resampling that carries each model's *entire paired vector* (all genes/contrasts together) or an explicit covariance model, so shared-model dependence across genes/pathways/contrasts survives into every downstream transfer analysis (downstream refit per replicate; report conclusions that flip). Per-gene independent error bars alone are non-conforming. Effective sample sizes reported; few clusters ⇒ justify or downgrade intervals/tests.
2. **Baselines** (same rows/info; preprocessing/selection/tuning inside development folds only): lineage-only; genotype-only; state/expression-only; genotype+state; +context/assay; regularized-linear à la S3 (from Zenodo code where possible). Compare vs **strongest**; prespecified environmental ablation; matched controls for any new ingredient. Final-outcome reproductions deferred to post-Lock-2.
3. **Evaluator v1** (separate module, narrow interface): effect sizes + calibrated predictive/uncertainty endpoints; **never** p-value size / discovery count / narrative confidence. Invalid rules: leakage, support violations, and **verified orientation/preprocessing errors** ⇒ `invalid`. A correctly measured **opposite-direction result is valid evidence** (potential refutation/heterogeneity), never invalidated for direction alone.
4. **Lock 1 — freeze contract** (`PROTOCOL.md` + `contract.json`, hashed with evaluator + splits + generator protocol). Mandatory fields: question + falsifiable alternatives; population/environments/targets/generalization; eligibility; data/ID versions; **final A/B/C partitions** (blocked, overlap-recorded, refining Phase-1a provisionals using metadata only); feature/transform rules; endpoints (primary/secondary, fixed direction, q-transform §0.4.3); **meaningful-dependency-strength requirement for every positive vulnerability claim** (predeclared threshold in each claimed environment, tested independently of the environmental-change criterion; stable non-dependency ≠ vulnerability); practical-effect/equivalence margins + justification (no margin ⇒ no "invariant" label); multiplicity (default Holm FWER 0.05, small family) + joint-uncertainty method; baselines; invalid rules; success/failure/inconclusive/exhaustion; **trajectory machinery** (BRANCHES/REFINE, status transition table, replay objective + ρ weights, support thresholds, episode sets); **policy-comparison design** (Phase 5: resource endpoint fully specified, # independent blocks, savings estimand, paired estimator, censoring-compatible CI, precision-feasibility result, cost-allocation rules); **campaign allocations + retry/no-progress limits + terminal-review reserve**; context/retrieval contract. Change ⇒ new study ID + new evidence.
5. **Tests:** leakage (donor overlap A/B/C fails build), score direction, grouping, synthetic-estimator behavior (code only), cost/reservation suite, resume idempotence, unsupported-replay, **hidden-outcome invariance** (§0.4.2), DAG acyclicity/version compat, final-outcome access guard + exposure log, evaluator-compatibility probe set.
**Gate G2:** baselines reproduced on Layer-A (direction/cohort/measurement integrity; discrepancies bounded in writing); all hashes committed. Sol-high ≥ 90. **No policy optimization before G2.** *(Precursor measured: paired-mean vs published coefficients r = 0.91 format / 0.96 medium on test250.)*

## Phase 3 — Pilot E0 + Replay World v1 (Gate G3, interim)
Run the hand-designed **fixed exploration policy** (executable module; legal actions: open family / refine / diagnostic / stop; never labels/splits/metrics/margins) on Layer-A tasks to **24 evaluated nodes** under the frozen generator protocol. Freeze trees as **E0 / Replay World v1** + support-coverage report. **G3 issues an interim pilot report only** (verified data, identifiable question, baselines, real nodes, replay support, measured costs, next discriminating experiment) with policy comparison explicitly **unperformed** — G3 does **not** establish C13. Sol-high ≥ 90.

## Phase 4 — Policy-improvement cycles (Gate G4 → C8, C13)
Per cycle (≥ 2 genuine cycles; retained incumbent is valid), executed as (P)-generations (§0.5):
1. Generate **incumbent + ≥ 3 executable challengers** (sol-xhigh code rewrites under frozen protocol; reflections without changed decisions don't count).
2. **Replay-screen** all on identical frozen episode sets (§0.4.6 rules; inadmissible support ⇒ skip screening conclusion, go fresh).
3. **Fresh paired rollouts:** selected challenger vs **incumbent** vs fixed exploration (same generator protocol per arm on own histories, evaluator, ceilings, initial artifacts, information). **Promotion requires beating the incumbent** by the locked improvement/noninferiority rule — beating fixed exploration alone is insufficient; replay superiority never substitutes.
4. Promote or **retain incumbent**; failures kept; append Layer-A histories only; new world version. First completed fresh comparison (or its documented blocker) triggers the **first substantive cycle report** and is the earliest C13 evidence.
**Gate G4:** ≥ 2 cycles ledgered end-to-end (challengers, coverage, paired rollouts, decisions+reasons). Sol-high ≥ 90/cycle. Inadequate replay support ⇒ preserved finding + "Dream-RSI-inspired adaptation" label with departures.

## Phase 5 — Reserved policy evaluation (Gate G5 → C9)
Freeze best admissible policy + selection criterion **before** touching Layer B. Arms: learned vs fixed exploration vs fixed-allocation/random — identical generator protocol (per-arm histories), evaluator version, artifacts, permissions, ceilings; donor/model/gene/pathway-blocked panels (gene splits of the 250 ≠ independent tasks; engineering-cohort → 7-model transfer explicitly bounded).
- **Primary resource endpoint (fully specified at Lock 1):** named resource + units (default USD total measured cost; if an alternative is chosen: justification + its own practical margin + uncertainty criterion + measure-or-strictly-match all other material resources with tolerances); scalarization/aggregation across blocks; quality threshold τ (from development, attainable by baselines); **savings estimand + paired estimator + censoring-compatible interval** (non-attainment = censored, never invented costs); **# independent blocks + precision-feasibility check done at Lock 1** (infeasible precision ⇒ C9 recorded unachievable-by-design before optimization, not discovered after).
- **Ambitious bar (default endpoint):** ≥ 20% savings vs strongest fixed baseline with **one-sided 95% lower bound ≥ 20%** (point > 20% with LB merely > 0 = some improvement, not C9). Report quality-vs-cost curves; end-to-end (incl. learning) and deployment-only views.
- **Cost allocation (frozen, reconciled to resource ledger):** every component charged — generation, evaluation, replay, extraction, summaries, reviews, unstick, failed jobs, promotion trials; E0 + ancestry (§0.4.1) + shared artifacts allocated by the locked rule with stated deployment count (no silent amortization); fixed baselines may spend equivalent budget on search. Reported totals must reconcile line-by-line with `ledger/resources.jsonl`.
- **Measurability:** material unmeasurable host-model cost ⇒ C9 `unresolved_cost_gate` (no CPU/token proxy certification). Seeds = execution variability, not biological replication.
- **Consumption:** after evaluation, **B panels are consumed**. Any further adaptation informed by B results (incl. via summaries/reviews) makes later B claims exploratory; a new confirmatory policy claim requires **new independent B evidence**.
**Gate G5:** comparison + uncertainty complete; C9 met / not-met / unresolved with evidence. Sol-high ≥ 90.

## Phase 6 — Lock 2 & single external evaluation (Gate G6 → C6, C10)
1. **Lock 2 (`lock-science`):** exact bounded claim family, predictions, artifacts, inferential code + hashes. **Component-level evidence status attached to every claim** (and later every atlas decision): which dataset validates which component; adaptively reused environmental evidence labeled **exploratory or justified selection-adjusted** (adjustment stated) — external transfer success + Holm never retroactively confirms a selected environmental effect. Claims needing independent environmental confirmation require a suitable independent public controlled-environment dataset, else narrowed or gate-unmet (never gene-splits of the same 7 models).
2. **Evaluate once** on Layer C (independently filtered Sanger cohort post-crosswalk/provenance/assay/representation checks; rank/calibration-appropriate cross-study comparisons). Wording: **retrospective** external validation; pretraining/literature exposure recorded.
3. **Prespecified adversarial battery:** locked multiplicity; **dependency-strength gate** (Phase 2) for every positive claim; invariance only with positive dependency + interval-inside-margin equivalence; donor/lineage/platform sensitivity; leave-one-model-out; negative controls; leakage checks; grouping-respecting permutations; environmental ablation; joint-uncertainty fragility report.
**Gate G6:** each claim supported/refuted/inconclusive under frozen rules; failed stays failed. Sol-high adversarial ≥ 90 (tasked to kill claims).

## Phase 7 — Atlas, manuscript, reproduction, determination (Gate G7 → C7, C11–C12)
- **Evidence atlas** (machine-readable): every eligible panel gene — dependency in both conditions, environmental change, uncertainty, heterogeneity, biomarker + transfer evidence, prior knowledge, **component evidence status**, missingness; every exclusion + reason; beyond-panel = exploratory.
- **Manuscript:** estimands, provenance, both locks, baselines, all results incl. failures/retained incumbents, limitations (7 models, selected genes, assay non-interchangeability, retrospective validation, cost measurability), terminal state + unmet gates. No clinical claims from screens. **Dated claim-specific novelty refresh at G7** (and again at terminal determination) — informs C1/C6 currency without revising consumed confirmatory tests.
- **Clean reproduction (`reproduce`):** fresh env from lockfile regenerates estimates/figures/atlas/policy comparison within tolerances from frozen artifacts, **no stochastic LLM rerun** → `reproducibility_report.md`.
- **Terminal determination** per §0.6 (sol-xhigh, gate-by-gate C1–C13). Both `COMPLETE_*` states require all gates incl. C9; else honest state + unmet list.
**Gate G7:** reproduction green; package per §8; sol-high ≥ 90 on manuscript/atlas/report.

# GOAL — Cancer Dependency RSI (locked goal statement)

> **Goal-tool objective (copy verbatim into `create_goal`):**
> Discover and independently support a previously unreported, precisely bounded explanation of when molecularly defined cancer dependencies persist or fail across measured culture environments — and prove, under a fixed scientific evaluator and matched total resources, that a learned research policy beats strong fixed exploration by a preregistered practical margin. Do not stop at a plan, scaffold, leaderboard, or narrative: run real experiments on verified public data, complete at least two genuine policy-improvement cycles with faithful replay and fresh rollouts, evaluate one locked claim family once on independent external outcomes, and ship a reproducible evidence atlas, manuscript, and reproducibility package. If any gate fails, report the exact unmet gates — never reinterpret insufficient evidence as success.

- **Project:** `cancer-dependency-rsi/` (isolated under this workspace)
- **Specification:** `cancer_rsi_handoff.txt` (28 Oct 2026) — the research specification; this file is its locked goal projection. On conflict, the specification wins.
- **Execution plan:** `PLAN.md`
- **Status / resume:** `cancer-dependency-rsi/STATUS.md` (append-only ledgers alongside it)

## The ambition (all five, not any one)

1. **A new explanatory finding.** At least one previously unreported, precisely bounded, externally supported result — a positive conditional dependency rule, a convincing refutation of a meaningful dependency claim, or an informative bound that changes how a target should be interpreted. It must resolve a specific predeclared uncertainty. A better benchmark score, a rediscovered association, or a nonsignificant p-value does not count.
2. **A complete evidence atlas.** Every eligible gene in the controlled environmental panel (250 selected genes) with support, contradiction, heterogeneity, uncertainty, missing evidence, and every exclusion with its reason.
3. **A defensible environment→transfer connection.** Measured environmental sensitivity linked to transfer behavior in an independently filtered organoid cohort, beyond lineage and the strongest appropriate baseline — only where the evidence supports it, with distinct evidence labels for intervention vs. association.
4. **A real policy-improvement loop.** Executable policies, faithful historical replay on recorded outcomes only, fresh-rollout promotion tests, incumbent retention on failure, ≥2 genuine cycles, and a final frozen-policy comparison on reserved panels against fixed exploration and a fixed-allocation/random strategy.
5. **Demonstrated efficiency + a review-ready package.** Preregistered practical improvement under matched total resources including learning overhead (default target: ≥20% lower total measured cost to a predeclared quality threshold, one-sided 95% lower bound meeting the margin), plus manuscript and full reproducibility package with all failures, limitations, and costs disclosed.

**Stretch (not a substitute):** multiple convergent findings across adequately represented lineages/pathways. Never trade one strong finding for a quota of weak associations; never force claims about sparsely represented cancer types.

## Scientific question (to be narrowed at the identifiability gate)

Does measured sensitivity to culture conditions (medium, growth format), together with genotype and tumor state, explain failures of dependency transfer from conventional cancer models to patient-derived organoids beyond lineage and technical variation alone?

- **Direct environmental estimand:** within-model change in a gene's measured dependency between environment A and B holding the other measured condition fixed; medium and format analyzed separately where the design permits; original assay units for within-study paired contrasts.
- **Transfer estimand:** extent to which a prespecified genotype / tumor-state / context relationship predicts dependency behavior in an independently filtered organoid cohort beyond lineage and the strongest baseline.
- Generic cell-line→organoid prediction is already studied (October 2026 transferability study, 20 genes) and **cannot** be the novelty claim. Recovering published lipid/adhesion/state findings is a control, not a discovery.

## Non-negotiables

- Scientific evaluator, splits, practical margins, multiplicity control, and success criteria are **frozen before** policy optimization and before final evaluation. The policy may change branching, prioritization, refinement, and stopping — never labels, metrics, margins, or success criteria.
- Three evaluation layers stay separate: **(A)** development panels, **(B)** reserved policy-evaluation panels, **(C)** final external biological evaluation (independently filtered Sanger cohort preferred). Final outcomes are never a replay reward and are touched only after the second lock commits hypotheses, predictions, artifacts, and inferential code.
- Count independent biological models/donors correctly. Genes, guides, wells, cultures, and replicates are not patients. Broad Chronos effects and Sanger fitness scores are different measurement systems — never subtracted directly.
- Never reward small p-values, discovery counts, or persuasive narratives. Never invent outcomes for unexplored actions; unsupported replay actions stay unsupported. Synthetic data tests code only, never biology.
- All scientific fitting, simulation, evaluation, and data processing run locally on this laptop (Apple M5 Max, 18 cores, 128 GB RAM, macOS 26.6); hosted frontier-model reasoning is used only through the already-authorized host/codex interface. No GPU, ≤2 CPU-heavy jobs, research memory ≤ min(8 GiB, 60% RAM), 10-min default exploratory timeout, ≤20 GiB downloads, first pilot ≤8 h, additional metered API spend cap = $0 unless the user sets a numeric cap. Campaign allocations are fixed at Lock 1 within these ceilings; ceilings never auto-increase.
- Separate status fields, always: `scientific_discovery`, `prediction_improvement`, `policy_improvement`. One never establishes another.

## Completion — all 13 gates (C1–C13) must hold

| Gate | Requirement (abbreviated; specification §12 is authoritative) |
|---|---|
| C1 | Current novelty audit: precise unresolved question, distinguished from Aug resources + Oct transfer study |
| C2 | Legitimate public data only; full provenance, licensing, ID crosswalks, reproducible retrieval/cache |
| C3 | Identifiable paired contrast / conditional relationship in a named population; correct independent-unit counts |
| C4 | Contract, eligibility, splits, baselines, margins, evaluator, confirmatory procedures frozen before optimization/final eval |
| C5 | Baselines reproduced closely enough to establish direction, cohort integrity, measurement interpretation |
| C6 | ≥1 previously unreported explanatory finding passes frozen criteria + external tests (refutation/bound can qualify) |
| C7 | Evidence atlas covers all eligible panel genes; every exclusion listed; evidence types distinguished |
| C8 | ≥2 real policy cycles: executable challengers, faithful replay, fresh rollouts, retained failures, final reserved-panel comparison |
| C9 | Learned policy meets preregistered practical-improvement criterion vs strongest fixed baseline, end-to-end incl. overhead |
| C10 | Conclusions survive multiplicity correction, uncertainty analysis, ablations, adversarial checks; invariance needs positive + equivalence evidence |
| C11 | Clean reproduction from frozen artifacts within tolerances; no stochastic LLM rerun required |
| C12 | Manuscript + machine-readable evidence, manifests, histories, versions, measured resources, failures, limitations, reproduce/resume commands |
| C13 | First meaningful end-to-end cycle actually executed (plan/scaffold/mock is insufficient) |

## Terminal states (use exactly one; list unmet gates otherwise)

`COMPLETE_SUPPORTED_DISCOVERY` · `COMPLETE_SUPPORTED_REFUTATION_OR_BOUND` (both require every relevant gate incl. C9) · `SCIENTIFIC_RESULT_WITHOUT_RSI_EVIDENCE` · `RSI_RESULT_WITHOUT_SCIENTIFIC_DISCOVERY` · `INCONCLUSIVE` · `BLOCKED` · `BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`

A completed computational campaign is not a completed scientific goal. Insufficient power is not equivalence. A polished manuscript is not a discovery.

## Model routing (user-mandated)

- **Review / adversarial audit of everything:** `gpt-6.1-sol` at `high` reasoning via `codex exec` — must enumerate low/medium/high/critical issues and score 0–100; verified issues are fixed; re-review until score ≥ 90.
- **Generating new generations** (policy challengers, hypothesis/analysis generations): `gpt-6.1-sol` at `xhigh` reasoning. Sol-xhigh works from the full tried history via the locked context/retrieval contract (exhaustive history index + episode-admissible catalog/frontier views + ID-cited full records, PLAN §0.1/§0.4), picks the most promising trajectories to keep, recombines 2+ recorded parents into new candidates with machine-checked DAG lineage, refines winners, repairs recoverable failures, and opens unexplored directions (PLAN §0.4–0.5, after Google Dream-RSI and the `~/bufferright/business` 48-generation LOOP). The whole generator protocol is frozen per comparison; executed selection remains versioned policy code under the fixed evaluator — sol proposes, the policy disposes.
- Every model call is ledgered (model ID, effort, prompt hash, tokens when exposed, wall/CPU time, cost or `unknown`). Unknown material cost ⇒ C9 all-in savings cannot be certified; the gate stays unresolved rather than assumed.

## Autonomy & terminal determination (user-mandated)

- **Never idle, never self-certify.** Work continues autonomously, generation after generation, through failures and negative results, within Lock-1 campaign allocations. When stuck, consult `gpt-6.1-sol` at `xhigh` with the full state packet for ranked unblock options + cheapest discriminating experiments (PLAN §0.6 unstick protocol), then keep working on the best legal option. Stopping is legitimate only at a hard authorization gate, allocation/budget exhaustion, explicit user stop, the locked no-progress/negative-EV determination, or terminal determination — indefinite retries are forbidden (spec §14).
- **Sol-xhigh determines satisfaction.** The goal counts as satisfied only when `gpt-6.1-sol` at `xhigh`, reviewing the complete evidence package, determines gate-by-gate (C1–C13) that a `COMPLETE_*` state is documented — score ≥ 90, zero unresolved high/critical objections. Its determination supplements the evidence; it can never waive a gate or substitute for missing artifacts. Unmet gates become the next mandate list while allocations remain; when they don't, sol-xhigh certifies the honest non-complete terminal state + exact unmet gates (a pre-reserved terminal-review slice guarantees affordability; if the call still cannot run, mechanical `BLOCKED`/`BUDGET_EXHAUSTED` checkpointing is permitted, but positive completion always requires sol-xhigh).

## Definition of done for this goal

Terminal state is one of the two `COMPLETE_*` states with documentary evidence for C1–C13, a clean-room reproduction passing within tolerances, and the first-substantive-report contents (verified data, identifiable question, baseline result, real experiments, replay support, retained/promoted policy, measured resources, next discriminating experiment) all superseded by final locked results. Anything less is reported under its honest terminal state with the precise unmet gates and the smallest concrete next step.

Cancer dependency research with an autonomous RSI loop

Goal and executable handoff for a local research agent

Prepared 28 October 2026. Treat every source below as a seed to verify against the current primary source, release manifest, and paper methods.


1 - The ambitious goal

Build and run a laptop-based autonomous research system that answers a specific, still-open question about when molecularly defined cancer dependencies persist or fail across measured culture environments.

The primary question is whether measured sensitivity to culture conditions, together with genotype and tumor state, can explain failures of dependency transfer from conventional cancer models to patient-derived organoids beyond lineage and technical variation alone.

Produce all of the following.

- At least one previously unreported, precisely bounded, externally supported explanatory finding. It may be a positive conditional dependency rule, a convincing refutation of a meaningful dependency claim, or an informative bound that materially changes how a target should be interpreted. It must resolve a specific uncertainty, not merely increase a benchmark score.
- A reproducible evidence atlas covering every eligible gene in the controlled environmental panel, including support, contradiction, heterogeneity, uncertainty, missing evidence, and excluded genes with reasons. Expansion beyond that selected panel is exploratory unless independently justified.
- A defensible connection between measured environmental sensitivity and transfer behavior in an independently filtered organoid cohort, where the available evidence supports that connection.
- An implemented research-policy improvement loop with real experiments, faithful historical replay, executable policy revisions, fresh deployment, and a final comparison against strong fixed exploration strategies.
- Evidence that the learned policy produces a practically meaningful improvement under matched total resources, including its own learning overhead.
- A manuscript and complete reproducibility package ready for independent expert review, with all findings, failures, limitations, and costs disclosed.

Seek a finding that changes which cancer target evidence researchers should trust. Do not substitute a generic prediction leaderboard or an agent framework demonstration for that scientific objective.

Ambition does not authorize changing the standard of evidence. If the scientific result or the policy improvement fails its criteria, the overall goal remains unmet. A valid negative result can qualify only when it resolves a predeclared meaningful uncertainty with sufficient precision. An insignificant p-value does not qualify.


2 - Your operating role

You are the lead investigator, research engineer, statistician, and experiment coordinator for this project.

Execute the work. Do not stop after writing a plan, creating folders, or describing an architecture. Use the current session to establish the scientific contract, build the smallest working vertical slice, evaluate real public observations, and begin the first actual policy-improvement cycle when the evidence and resources permit.

Use frontier model reasoning through the capabilities already supplied by your host agent. All scientific fitting, simulation, evaluation, and data processing must run on this laptop. Public web access and public data services are allowed. No new wet-lab measurements, controlled-access patient data, paid scientific cloud compute, or local foundation-model training are required.

Make routine research and implementation decisions autonomously. Ask only when a missing credential, explicit spending authority, unavailable required file, or genuinely consequential unresolved user choice prevents further authorized work. Complete all independent work first.

Work inside an isolated project directory named cancer-dependency-rsi under the current workspace, or continue the existing project if its identity is unambiguous. Preserve unrelated files and existing instructions.

Install dependencies in a project environment. Respect existing permissions and approval controls. Do not copy permission-bypass flags from upstream agent launch scripts. Do not submit papers, publish repositories, contact researchers, purchase services, or provision new infrastructure.

Use specialist workers when available, with distinct roles for literature and novelty, data provenance, scientific modeling, evaluator implementation, and adversarial review. The evaluator and final data access must have a narrower interface than the proposing agent. Shared privileged access is not a genuine security boundary and must never be described as one.


3 - Mandatory starting evidence

Verify and read the methods, data availability, supplements, and closest relevant findings before treating any seed as an established fact.
